𒊬𒂵𒉌𒈗𒌷 𒈗 𒀀𒂵𒌤𒆠
Sar-ga-ni-sar-ali  šar  A-ga-de.KI
Sargon,  king of Agade

II. Narām-Sin, about 3750 B.C.
𒈾𒊏𒄠𒀭 𒂗𒍪  𒈗  𒆠𒅁𒊏𒁴 𒅈𒁀𒅎
Na-ra-am-ilu Sin  šar  ki-ib-ra-tim ar-ba-im
Narām-Sin,  king of the four quarters

III. Ḫammurabi, king of Babylon, about 2200 B.C.
𒄩𒄠𒈬𒊏𒁉  𒈗  𒆗   𒈗   𒆍𒀭𒊏𒆠
Ḫa-am-mu-ra-bi  šarru  dannu  šar  Bābili.KI
Ḫammurabi,  the mighty king,  king of Babylon

IV. Šamši-Rammān, of Assyria, about 1820 B.C.
𒌑𒅆𒀭    𒅎  𒉺𒋼𒋛  𒐀𒐼  𒌉
Šamši-ilu Rammān  iššakku  Aššur  mār
Šamši-Rammān,  ruler of Aššur,  son

𒅖𒈨𒀭    𒆠𒍇   𒉺𒋼𒋛  𒀭𒀀𒋩
Iš-me-ilu Dagan  iššakku  ilu A-šur
of Išme-Dagan,  ruler of Aššur

V. Pudi-ilu, king of Assyria, about 1350 B.C.
𒁍𒁲𒀭  𒈗  𒆳  𒐀𒐼   𒀀  𒀭  𒂗𒉌𒊏𒊑  𒈗
Pu-di-ilu  šar mātu Aššur  apil ilu Bēl-nirari  šar
Pudi-ilu,  king of Assyria,  son of Bēl-nirari,  king

𒆳  𒐀𒐼  𒀀  [Aššur-uballiṭ]  𒈗  𒆳  𒐀𒐼
mātu Aššur  apil Aššur-uballiṭ  šar mātu Aššur
of Assyria,  son of Aššur-uballiṭ,  king of Assyria

VI. Rammān-nirari I, king of Assyria, about 1325 B.C.

𒀭𒂄𒈠𒉡𒀀𒊭𒊑𒁺  𒈗  𒊹  𒀀  𒅎𒉌𒊏𒊑
ilu Šulmānu-ašaridu  šar kiššati  apil Rammān-nirari

šar kiššati

IX. Nebuchadnezzar I, king of Babylonia, about 1150 B.C.
𒀭𒈾𒁍𒆪𒄙𒊑𒌋𒋩  𒊒𒁍  𒈾𒀀𒁺  𒈾
ilu Nabū-ku-dur-ri-uṣur  rubū  na-a-du  na-
Nebuchadnezzar,  the prince,  exalted (and)

𒀾𒆪  𒍣𒅋  𒆍𒆠  𒂊𒌀
as-ku și-il  Bābili.KI  e-til
magnificent,  offspring of Babylon,  the lord

𒈗(pl)  𒉺𒋼𒋛  [kardu]  𒊕𒅗𒅘𒆪
šarrāni(pl)  iššakku  kardu  šakkanakku
of kings,  the courageous ruler,  governor

𒌷𒉣  𒀭𒌓  𒆳𒋗
alu Eridu  ilu Šamaš  māti-šu
of Eridu,  the sun of his land

of Aššur-rēš-iši,  king of Assyria,  son of Mutak-

EXTRACTS FROM THE RECORDS OF TIGLATH-PILESER III.

I. The reduction of Hamath and the cities tributary to Uzziah of Judah, about 740 B.C.
[From paper squeezes in the British Museum, Nos. 115 a—115 e.]

1. [XIX]  𒈾𒄀𒂊  𒊭  𒌷 𒄩𒄠𒈠𒀜𒋾
XIX  na-gi-e  ša  alu Ha-am-ma-at-ti
Nineteen  districts  of  the city of Hamath

2. 𒀀𒁲  𒌷𒎌(ni)  𒊭  𒋛𒂡𒋾𒋢𒉡
a-di  alāni(pl)(ni)  ša  si-hir-ti-šu-nu
together with  the cities  round about them

3. 𒊭  𒀀𒄭  𒌓𒁴  𒊭  [šulmu](mu)  𒀭𒌓(ši)
ša  a-hi  tam-tim  ša  šulmu(mu)  ilu Šamši(ši)
which (are)(on the shore of)  the sea  of  the setting  of the sun

4. 𒊭  𒄿𒈾  𒄭𒀉𒋾  𒌋  𒆰𒈜𒋼
ša  i-na  hi-it-ti  u  kul-lul-te
which  in  sin  and  iniquity

5. 𒀀𒈾  𒁹𒊍𒊑𒅀𒀀𒌋  𒂊𒆠𒄿𒈬
a-na  m Az-ri-ia-a-u  e-ki-i-mu
to  Azariah (i.e., Uzziah)  they had detached

6. 𒀀𒈾  𒈪𒋓  𒆳𒐀𒐼  𒌋𒌁𒊏𒀀
a-na  mi-sir  matu Aššur  utir-ra-a
to  the territory of  Assyria  I brought back

7. 𒇻  𒋗𒌓𒊭𒆥(pl)-𒅀  𒇻  𒂗-[paḫāti](pl)
amēlu  šu-ud-šaqê(pl)-ia  amēlu  bēl-paḫāti(pl)
My officers  ...  as governors

8. 𒌋𒅗-𒋗𒉡  𒀸𒆲  [30,300]  𒌦𒎌
eli-šunu  aškun  XXX.M,III.C  niše(pl)
over them  I appointed  30,300  people

9. 𒀾𒋢𒄩𒄠𒈠  [ultu]  𒆠𒆗
as-su-ha-am-ma  ultu  ki-rib
I carried away  from  the midst of

10. 𒌷𒎌-𒋗𒉡-𒈠  [piḫāt]  𒌷  𒆪 [illegible]
alāni(pl)-šunu-ma  piḫāt  alu  Ku.....
their cities  and  in the district of  Ku.....

11. 𒌋𒊭𒀾𒂍
u-ša-aṣ-bit
I settled.

INSCRIPTION OF DARIUS, KING OF BABYLON, 521-485 B.C.
[From Schulz, Journal Asiatique, Troisième Série, t. IX, pl. VIII, col. a.]

1.  𒀭  𒃲  𒀀𒌨𒊒𒈠𒊍𒁕
1.  ilu  rabū(u)  ilu A-ḫu-ru-ma-az-da
A great god  (is)  Ahuramazda,

2.  𒊭  𒆕𒅗𒊒  𒀀𒂵𒀀  3.  𒀉𒁷𒉡  𒊭
2.  ša  kak-ka-ru  a-ga-a  3.  id-din-nu  ša
who  this soil  did create,  who

𒊭𒈨𒂊  4.  𒀭𒉡𒌅  𒀉𒁷𒉡  𒊭
šamê(e)  4.  an-nu-tu  id-din-nu  ša
these heavens  did create,  who

𒇻  𒂞𒎌  𒀉𒁷𒉡  6.  𒊭  𒃮𒁉
amēlu  șābē(pl)  id-din-nu  6.  ša  gab-bi
mankind  did create,  who  all

𒉡𒄴𒋢  7.  𒀀𒈾  𒇻  𒂞𒎌  𒀉𒁷𒉡
nu-uḫ-šu  7.  a-na  amēlu  șābē(pl)  id-din-nu
abundance  unto  mankind  has granted,

8.  𒊭  𒀀𒈾  𒁹  𒁕𒀀𒊑𒅀𒀀𒈲  9.  𒈗
8.  ša  a-na  m  Da-a-ri-ia-a-muš  9.  šarri
who  to  Darius  the king

𒅁𒉡𒌋  10.  [išten(en)]  𒄿𒈾  𒈗𒎌𒉌  𒈤𒊒𒌅
ib-nu-u  10.  išten(en)  ina  šarrāni(pl)  maḫ-ru-tu
did make  one  among  kings  that were before,

[anaku]  13.  𒁹 𒁕𒀀𒊑𒅀𒀀𒈲  𒈗

of  the whole of

all tongues,

17.  𒈗  𒊭  𒆕𒅗𒊒  𒀀𒂵𒀀𒋫

3. A prayer for forgiveness.

8. A prayer for restoration to divine favour.

1. Hymn to the Rising Sun.

[K 3343.]

1.  𒀭  𒊭𒈠𒌍  𒄿𒈾  𒄿[šid]  𒊭𒈨𒂊  𒋫𒁍𒄩𒄠𒈠

šamê(e)  ma-ta-a-ti  tak-tum²
of heaven  the lands  thou hast covered!

4. Some reasons for a curse falling upon a man.

[K 150 and K 8868.]

1.  𒀀𒈾  𒂍  𒋰𒉿𒂊𒋗  𒄿𒋼𒊒𒌒
1.  a-na  bīt  tap-pi-e-šu  i-te-ru-ub
Into  the house of  his neighbour  has he entered?

2.  𒀀𒈾  [aššat]  𒋰𒉿𒂊𒋗  𒀉𒋼𒄭 ²
2.  a-na  aššat  tap-pi-e-šu  it-te-hi ²

3.  [dāmē pl]  𒋰𒉿𒂊𒋗  𒀉𒋫[bak]
3.  dāmē pl  tap-pi-e-šu  it-ta-bak

4.  [șubat]  𒋰𒉿𒂊𒋗  𒀉𒋫𒁄 ³
4.  șubat  tap-pi-e-šu  it-ta-bal ³
The garment of  his neighbour  has he stolen?

5.  𒈪𒊏𒉡𒍑𒋗  𒀉𒇻  𒆷  𒌋𒈦𒅆𒊒
5.  mi-ra-nu-uš-šu  id-lu  la  u-maš-ši-ru
From his power(?)  a man  has he not  let go free?

6.  𒀉𒇻  𒁮𒅗  𒀸  𒁶𒋾𒋗
6.  id-lu  dam-ka  ina  kim-ti-šu
A good man  from  his family

𒌋[še]𒇻𒌋  7.  𒆥𒈾  𒁍𒄯𒋫
u-še-lu-u  7.  kin-na  pu-hur-ta
has he driven away?  A united household

𒌋𒉺𒅁𒉿𒌨  8.  𒀀𒈾  𒆷𒁍[ut]𒋾𒄿
u-sap-pi-hu  8.  a-na  la-bu-ut-ti-i
has he broken up?  Against  one in authority

𒄑𒍝𒊍𒍮  9.  𒉿𒄿𒋗  𒋻𒍮
iz-za-az-zu  9.  pi-i-šu  tar-șu
has he set himself?  In his mouth  is he honest,

𒈜𒁀𒋗  𒆷  𒆠𒄿𒉌  10.  𒉿𒄿𒋗  𒀭𒈾
lib-ba-šu  la  ki-i-ni  10.  pi-i-šu  an-na
(but) in his heart  false!  With his mouth  (says he) yea,

𒈜𒁀𒋗  𒌌𒆷 ¹
lib-ba-šu  ul-la ¹
(but) in his heart  nay?

LETTERS FROM TELL EL-AMARNA.

1. Letter from Burraburiash, king of Karduniash, to Amenophis IV, king of Egypt, begging for larger presents and for his co-operation against the Assyrians.

[Bu. 88-10-13, 81.]

1.  𒀀𒈾  𒉌𒅁𒌨𒌨𒊑𒊑𒅀

ul-te-bi-i-lu  10.  u  me-ri-el-ta

Now

am-mi-ni  el-li-ku-u-ni
why  are they come?

34.  𒋳𒈠  𒋫𒊏𒄴𒈠𒀭𒉌
34.  šum-ma  ta-ra-ah-ma-an-ni
If  thou lovest me

𒅆𒈠𒀀𒋾  𒈪𒅎𒈠
ši-ma-a-ti  mi-im-ma
any business

35.  𒆷
35.  la
let them not

𒅁𒁍𒌋𒋗  𒊑𒆪𒋾𒋗𒉡
ip-pu-u-šu  ri-ku-ti-šu-nu
carry out,  their failure (in their enterprise)

𒆪𒍑𒅆𒁕𒋗𒉡𒋾
ku-uš-ši-da-šu-nu-ti
secure.

36.  𒀀𒈾  𒋗𒌌𒈠𒉌𒅗  III  𒈠𒈾
36.  a-na  šu-ul-ma-ni-ka  III  ma-na
As  a present for thee  3  manehs

[abnu]  [ukni]  [šadi]  𒌋  V  [similtu]  𒊭
abnu  ukni  šadi  u  V  similtu  ša
of lapis lazuli  of the mountain  and  5  yoke  of

[sisē pl]  𒊭  V  𒄑  [narkabāt-iși pl]
sisē pl  ša  V  işu  narkabāt-iși pl
horses  for  5  wooden chariots

38.  𒌌𒋼𒁉𒆷𒀝𒆪
38.  ul-te-bi-la-ak-ku
have I sent thee.


2. Letter from Tushratta, king of Mitani, to Amenophis III, king of Egypt, sent with an image of the goddess Ishtar into Egypt.

[Bu. 88-10-13, 78.]

1.  𒀀𒈾  𒁹  𒉌𒉏𒈬𒊑𒅀
1.  a-na  m Ni-im-mu-ri-ia
To  Nimmuria,

𒈗  𒆳  [Mi-iș-ri-i]  2.  𒀀𒄭𒅀
šar  mātu  Mi-iș-ri-i  2.  ahi-ia
king  of Egypt,  my brother,

𒄩𒋫𒉌𒀀  𒊭  𒀀𒊏'𒀀𒈬
ha-ta-ni-a  ša  a-ra-'-a-mu
my son-in-law,  whom  I love

3.  𒌋  𒊭  𒄿𒊏'𒀀𒈠𒀭𒉌
3.  u  ša  i-ra-'-a-ma-an-ni
and  who  loves me,

𒆠𒁉𒈠  4.  [um-ma]  𒁹  𒁺𒍑𒋥𒋫
ki-bi-ma  4.  um-ma  m Du-uš-rat-ta
speaks  thus  Dušratta,

𒈗  𒈪𒄿𒋫𒀭𒉌  5.  𒊭
šar  Mi-i-ta-an-ni  5.  ša
king  of Mitani,  who

𒄿𒊏'𒀀𒈬𒅗  [emu]𒅗𒈠
i-ra-'-a-mu-ka  emu-ka-ma
loves thee,  thy father-in-law.

6.  𒀀𒈾  𒅀𒅆  𒂄𒈬  𒀀𒈾
6.  a-na  ia-ši  šul-mu  a-na
With  me  it is well;  with

𒅗𒀀𒊭  𒇻𒌋  𒂄𒈬  7.  𒀀𒈾
ka-a-ša  lu-u  šul-mu  7.  a-na
thee  may  it be well,  with

𒂍𒋾𒅗  𒀀𒈾  f  𒋫𒀀𒌈𒄭𒉺
bīti-ka  a-na  f Ta-a-tum-hi-pa
thy house,  with  Tātumhipa

[mārti]𒅀  8.  𒀀𒈾  [assāti]𒅗  𒊭
mārti-ia  8.  a-na  aššati-ka  ša
my daughter,  with  thy wife  whom

𒋫𒊏'𒀀𒈬  𒇻𒌋  𒂄𒈬
ta-ra-'-a-mu  lu-u  šul-mu
thou lovest  may it be well;

9.  𒀀𒈾  [assāti pl]𒅗  𒀀𒈾  𒈥𒎌𒅗
9.  a-na  assāti pl-ka  a-na  mārē pl-ka
with  thy wives,  with  thy sons,

𒀀𒈾  [amelūti pl]  𒃲𒎌𒅗  10.  𒀀𒈾
a-na  amelūti pl  rabūti-ka  10.  a-na
with  thy nobles,  with

𒄑  𒈜𒆏𒋾𒅗  𒀀𒈾  [sisē pl]𒅗
işu  narkabāti pl-ka  a-na  sisē pl-ka
thy chariots,  with  thy horses,

11.  𒀀𒈾  𒂞𒎌𒅗  𒀀𒈾  𒆳𒅗
11.  a-na  șābē pl-ka  a-na  māti-ka
with  thy troops,  with  thy land

𒌋  𒀀𒈾  12.  [mim]𒈬𒅗  𒆗𒎙
u  a-na  12.  mim-mu-ka  dan-niš
and  with  all that is thine  exceedingly
𒆗𒎙  𒆗𒎙  𒇻𒌋  𒂄𒈬
dannis(is)  dannis(is)  lu-u  šul-mu
exceedingly  exceedingly  well may it be.

13.  𒌝𒈠  𒀭𒈹  𒊭  𒌷𒉌𒄿𒈾𒀀
13.  um-ma  ilu Ištar  ša  alu Ni-i-na-a
Thus (spake)  Ishtar  of  Nineveh,

[bêlit]  𒆳𒎌
bêlit  mātāti
the lady  of all lands:

14.  𒃮𒁉𒄿𒅆𒈾𒈠  𒀀𒈾
14.  gab-bi-i-ši-na-ma  a-na
of all lands:  "Unto

𒆳  𒈪𒄑𒊑𒄿
mātu  Mi-iş-ri-i
Egypt

15.  𒄿𒈾  𒆳  𒊭
15.  i-na  māti  ša
into  the land  which

𒀀𒊏𒄴𒀀𒈬  𒇻𒌌𒌨𒆪𒈨
a-ra-'-a-mu  lu-ul-lik-ku-me
I love  I will go,

16.  𒇻[uş][şa]𒄭𒅕𒈨  𒀀𒉡𒌝𒈠
16.  lu-uş-şa-ḫi-ir-me  a-nu-um-ma
I will depart(?).  Verily

𒄿𒈾𒀭𒈾  17.  𒌌𒋼𒂊[bil]𒈠
i-na-an-na  17.  ul-te-e-bil-ma
now  have I sent (her)  and

𒀉𒊑𒅗
it-tal-ka
she is gone.

18.  𒀀𒉡𒌝𒈠  𒄿𒈾  𒌁[şi]
18.  a-nu-um-ma  i-na  tir-şi
Indeed  in  the time of

19.  𒀀𒁉𒅀𒈠  𒀭𒈹  [beltum]  𒄿𒈾
19.  a-bi-ia-ma  ilu Ištar  bêltum(tum)  i-na
my father  Ištar,  the lady,  into

𒆳  𒊭𒀀𒅆  𒀉𒊑𒅗  20.  𒌋
māti  ša-a-ši  it-tal-ka  20.  u
that land  went  and

𒆠𒄿𒈨𒂊  𒄿𒈾  𒉺𒈾𒀀𒉡𒌝𒈠
ki-i-me-e  i-na  pa-na-a-nu-um-ma
just as  formerly

21.  𒀉𒋫[šab]𒈠  𒊌𒋼𒅁𒁉𒁺𒅆¹
21.  it-ta-šab-ma  uk-te-ib-bi-du-ši¹
she dwelt (there)  and  they honoured her

22.  [u]  𒄿𒈾𒀭𒈾  𒀀𒄭𒅀
22.  u  i-na-an-na  ahi-ia
so  now  may my brother

𒀀𒈾  [X-šu]  23.  𒌋𒅗  𒊭  𒉺𒈾𒀀𒉡
a-na  X-šu  23.  eli  ša  pa-na-a-nu
ten times  more than  formerly

𒇷𒄀𒅁𒁉𒅖𒋛²  24.  𒀀𒄭𒅀
li-gi-ib-bi-is-si²  24.  ahi-ia
honour her.  May my brother

𒇷𒄀𒅁𒁉𒅖𒍮²  𒄿𒈾  𒄩𒁲𒂊
li-gi-ib-bi-is-zu²  i-na  ḫa-di-e
honour her,  with  joy

25.  𒇷[miš]𒋓𒋗𒈠  𒇷𒁺𒌋𒊏³
25.  li-miš-šir-šu-ma  li-du-u-ra³
may he allow her  to return.

1. II 2 Pret. from kabātu with pron. suffix.
2. II 1 Prec. from kabātu with pron. suffix.  3. I 1 Prec. from tāru.

26.  𒀭𒈹  [bêlit]  𒊭𒈨𒂊  𒀀𒄭𒅀
26.  ilu Ištar  bêlit  ša-me-e  ahi-ia
May Ishtar  the lady of  heaven,  my brother

𒌋  𒅀𒅆  27.  𒇷𒄑𒋩𒀭𒈾𒅆
u  ia-ši  27.  li-iş-şur-an-na-ši
and  me  protect

[I.C]  𒇷𒅎  [šanāti pl]  28.  𒌋
I.C  li-im  šanāti(pl)  28.  u
for 100,000  years  and

𒄭𒁺𒋫  𒊏𒁉𒋫  [bêlit]  [işāti]
ḫi-du-ta  ra-bi-ta  bêlit  işāti
great joy  may the lady  of fire

29.  𒀀𒈾  𒆠𒇷𒀀𒀠𒇷𒉌
29.  a-na  ki-la-a-al-li-ni
unto  both of us

𒇷𒀉𒁷𒀭𒈾𒅆𒈠  30.  𒌋
li-id-din-an-na-ši-ma  30.  u
give  and

𒆠𒄿  𒋫𒀀𒁉  𒄿  𒉌𒁍𒍑²
ki-i  ta-a-bi  i  ni-pu-uš²
thus  good  shall we do.

1. ta-a-bi = ṭābi.
2. The last two lines of the letter, the meaning of which is uncertain, are here omitted.
